# PROJECT MEDOS — Worklog
AI-Powered MBBS → NEET-PG Learning Operating System

## Product Architecture (final decisions)
- **Constraint**: Single Next.js page route `/` (SPA with Zustand view routing). All backend = `/api/*` routes.
- **Stack**: Next.js 16 + TypeScript + Tailwind 4 + shadcn/ui + Framer Motion + Prisma/SQLite + z-ai-web-dev-sdk (backend only).
- **Design**: "Medical OS" premium dark-first theme — deep midnight navy, clinical cyan primary, amber/red severity, glass surfaces, large typography. Light mode supported.
- **Views**: landing | onboarding | home | map | learn | questions | cases | revise | tutor | progress | roadmap | profile (+ concept explorer overlay + global search).
- **Knowledge engine** (`src/lib/engine.ts`): Ebbinghaus forgetting curve (R = e^(-t/S)), per-concept KnowledgeState, spaced repetition (SM-2-lite), NEXT_BEST_ACTION scoring (recall risk + weakness + exam relevance + year match + error patterns).
- **Content**: All-original educational content. Subjects per NMC CBME structure (19 subjects), concepts as graph nodes with typed edges, ~60 original MCQs, ~50 flashcards, 4+ clinical cases, confusion pairs, disease/drug/investigation deep dives.
- **Task IDs**: 1 (schema), 1b (seed), 1c (libs), 1d (APIs), 2-a..2-f (parallel view subagents), 3 (shell wiring), 4 (QA), 5 (cron).

Task ID: 1/1b/1c/1d
Agent: main
Task: Schema, seed, libs, APIs

Work Log:
- Prisma schema: StudentProfile, Subject/Topic/Concept/ConceptEdge (graph), Question/Attempt, KnowledgeState, Flashcard/Review, RevisionItem, StudySession, ErrorPattern, ConfusionPair, ClinicalCase/CaseRun, ResourceLog
- Seed: 19 NMC-CBME subjects, 25 topics, 45 concepts (rich detail for flagship chains: RAAS, nephrotic/nephritic, ACS, DKA, Graves, TB), ~80 typed edges, 55 original MCQs, 64 flashcards, 4 progressive clinical cases, 6 confusion pairs, demo student "Arpit" (Y2, serious prep) w/ 30-day history
- Libs: types.ts, store.ts (zustand), engine.ts (forgetting curve R=e^(-t/1.6S), SRS, NEXT_BEST_ACTION scoring, exam clock, roadmap builder), api.ts client, graph-layout.ts force layout
- APIs: profile, dashboard, graph (scoped), concepts/[id] (why-chain assembled), subjects, questions, attempts (+error-type → confusion detection), revision (+review SRS, +clear), cases (+step, +complete), tutor (z-ai SDK, 7 modes, safety rules), roadmap, progress (heatmap, weekly report, confusions), search (synonyms/abbrevs), sessions. All smoke-tested 200.

Stage Summary:
- Backend complete and verified. Frontend views next: shared context = use `@/lib/api`, `@/lib/types`, `@/lib/store`; theme classes in globals.css (.glass, .med-grid, sev-ok/warn/crit, ink-soft); shadcn/ui in src/components/ui; framer-motion + recharts available; single-page app — navigation via useAppStore.setView, concept drill-in via openConcept.

- Section 1 greeting: "{greeting}, Dr. {name}." (text-3xl md:text-4xl) + stage chip "{stageLabel} · {PREP_STAGE_LABELS[prepStage]}"; NEET-PG CLOCK row: Timer icon, "Estimated exam window: June {examYear} · {daysLeft} days left · stage: {stage}" + NBEMS disclaimer
- Section 2 intelligence strip: 5 glass stat tiles (grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-5) — Flame/warn topicsAtRisk, AlertTriangle/crit recurringMistakes, Target/crit weaknesses.length, BookOpen/info dueQuestions, Brain/muted dueFlashcards; bottom row: recommended minutes + Zap streak badge + mini brain-score ring
- Section 3: custom SVG brain-score ring (framer-motion stroke-dashoffset, cyan gradient #22d3ee→#0284c7, -rotate-90) + knowledge split stacked bar (animated widths, sev-ok/warn/crit/muted segments) with counts legend and weekly accuracy delta (TrendingUp/Down, sev colors)
- Section 4 TODAY'S MISSION: "≈ sum min" badge, progress bar (heatToday.minutes/recommendedMinutes), plan segment rows (index, minutes chip, activity, detail, hover), START TODAY'S MISSION → setQuizPreset({conceptId: nextAction.conceptId, count: 5}) + setView('questions') (falls back to setView('progress') when no nextAction); OPEN IN REVISE → setView('revise')
- Section 5 NEXT BEST ACTION: border-l-4 border-l-primary glass, eyebrow with activityType, title, italic "Because: {reason}", duration chip, horizontal mini-plan steps, START {duration} MIN BLOCK → setQuizPreset count 6 + questions; "Why this?" → openConcept; "View on Medical Map" → setView('map'); null-state = knowledge-audit card → setView('progress')
- Section 6: revision debt card (big AnimatedNumber count, ≈ minutes, FIX NOW → revise, zero-debt positive state) + top weaknesses list (clickable rows → openConcept, animated mastery bars with sev-tone mapping, "Full progress →" → progress)
- Motion: staggered Reveal sections (0.06s), AnimatedNumber counters via framer-motion animate() (once on load), ProgressRing/Bar gated by useReducedMotion (renders final state instantly when reduced); all buttons/rows min-h-11 (44px); lucide icons only, no emojis

  - CONFIG: "QUESTION LAB" header + exact subtitle; Subject Select (api.subjects, loading skeleton + error retry), System Select (SYSTEMS), Count slider 5–30 step 5 default 10, mode chips (All types/rapid/vignette); START SESSION
  - quizPreset auto-start: mount effect reads store preset, defers via setTimeout(0) (keeps react-hooks set-state-in-effect clean), presetStartedRef double-start guard, setQuizPreset(null) claimed inside the start tick, count default 6; "Focused practice" banner in config+run with concept name fetched via api.concept
  - RUN: top bar Q{i+1}/{n} + segmented progress bar (segments color by result correctness) + ticking mm:ss timer + X exit → AlertDialog "End session? Progress is saved per question." (partial results → RESULTS, zero → CONFIG); AnimatePresence mode="wait" slide between questions; meta row = difficulty dots ●●○ + qtype + subject + system chips; stem text-lg leading-relaxed; full-width radio option cards (≥44px, selected border-primary bg-primary/10, post-attempt correct=sev-ok/wrong pick=sev-crit + icons, A–D letters); keyboard 1–4/A–D (window keydown, input-guarded, disabled after submit); confidence Slider 1–5 Guess→Certain default 3; SUBMIT → api.attempt({questionId,selected,timeMs:per-question,confidence}) with inline error+retry
  - Verdict: correct = green banner + explanation card (CheckCircle2 header, teaching line border-l-primary highlighted); incorrect = red banner + correct-answer text + "WHY DID YOU MISS THIS?" 9 ERROR_TYPES cards (label+hint) → click POSTs api.logErrorType w/ "logged" chip, errorTypeSuggestion pre-selected, skippable → NEXT QUESTION / SEE RESULTS
  - RESULTS: logSession({minutes:max(1,round(sec/60)),kind:'questions',label:'Quiz session'}) fire-once (ref guard) incl. early exit; animated SVG score ring (accuracy%), correct/total, avg s/question, session length; by-difficulty bars (Easy/Moderate/Hard); knowledge note when AttemptResult.mastery present ("Knowledge state updated: 48% → unstable"); missed list w/ stems + correct answer + teaching one-liners (flawless zero-state); RUN ANOTHER SET → CONFIG, BACK TO DASHBOARD → setView('home'); run 'empty' filter state handled
- Built src/components/cases/cases-view.tsx ('use client', export CasesView) — ONLY file, list/player internal states:
  - LIST: "CLINICAL CASE SIMULATOR" header + exact subtitle; md:grid-cols-2 cards: difficulty chip (Easy/Moderate/Hard sev-toned), specialty chip, title, patient line (age/sex/occupation — complaint), attempted + mini last-score ring (static SVG, sev-toned) vs "New case" Sparkles, START CASE; api.cases() refetched on return for fresh scores
  - PLAYER: header w/ back button + case title + chips; lg:grid-cols-[320px_1fr], mobile chart stacks above; sticky PATIENT CHART (initials avatar from title, age/sex/occupation + extra patient rows, complaint quote, FINDINGS SO FAR derived from revealed steps w/ phase tags, max-h-64 overflow-y-auto); phase stepper from real step.phase values (completed=check+sev-ok, active=primary, ChevronRight separators, overflow-x-auto); AnimatePresence step card: phase badge + "Step x of y" + title + content bullets staggered 0.12s (reduced-motion gated)
  - Decision panel "YOUR CALL": radio option cards (≥44px) + CONFIRM DECISION → api.caseStep(id,{stepId,choice}) → emerald "Good clinical reasoning" / rose "Missed it — here's why" + amber border-l teaching callout (Lightbulb); correct/wrong option highlighting via answerId; POST error → inline retry; steps w/o question render content + CONTINUE
  - REPORT (last step): content renders then "CLINICAL REASONING REPORT" card — api.caseComplete fired exactly once on reaching report (completeFiredRef; {correctSteps, totalSteps: question steps, detail:[{stepId,correct,chosen}]}) → animated score ring + per-step Correct/Missed/Skipped review + learning points (case.learning) + RETRY CASE (resets run state) / MORE CASES (→ list) / TURN INSIGHT INTO QUESTIONS (setQuizPreset({system: case.system, count: 8}) + setView('questions'))
- QA: bun run lint → 0 errors in my files (only pre-existing prisma/seed.ts warning); bunx tsc --noEmit → 0 errors in my files (remaining errors pre-exist in untouched api routes/seed); all setState kept out of effect bodies (.then patterns / interval / deferred preset tick)

Stage Summary:
- Artifacts: src/components/questions/quiz-view.tsx, src/components/cases/cases-view.tsx — no other files touched
- Decisions: quizPreset claimed+started inside setTimeout(0) tick with ref guard (strict-mode double-effect safe, avoids sync setState-in-effect); error-type logging only on explicit card click (pre-selected suggestion is UI-only, next = skip); exit-dialog "End session" routes to RESULTS with partial entries (0 answered → config); results array stores {questionId,correct,selected,timeMs,stem,answerText,teaching,difficulty,qtype} so breakdowns/teaching need no refetch; findings derived from revealed index (no effect); caseComplete fired on advancing into final step, retry button re-POSTs on failure only; case list refetch keyed on reloadKey when returning from player

Task ID: 9-a
Agent: visual3d-expander (subagent)
Task: Add 6 new hand-authored 3D diagrams to DIAGRAMS_3D registry in src/lib/visual3d.ts

Work Log:
- Read worklog.md (Tasks 6/7/8) + existing visual3d.ts conventions (entry shape, layer style, tint palette, z 0..N, simple/detail tone) before writing.
- Cross-checked every new diagram against the concept's own detail text in prisma/seed-data.ts (c-aki L265, c-dka L305, c-imnci L738, c-thyroidstorm L531, c-schizo-frs L819, c-sepsis L631) — all numbers pulled/consistent from those entries, restated in original words (no verbatim source text).
- 'c-aki' — 6 layers: prerenal (FENa <1%, BUN:Cr >20:1), ATN (muddy brown casts, FENa >2%), interstitial (WBC casts/eosinophiluria), postrenal (hydronephrosis), KDIGO staging (Cr ×1.5–1.9 / ×2–2.9 / ×3 or ≥4 or RRT or anuria ≥12 h), dialysis AEIOU.
- 'c-dka' — 6 layers: triad (glucose 300–600, AG >12, pH <7.3), bedside picture (Kussmaul, pseudoperitonitis), fluids first (1–1.5 L saline), insulin (0.1 U/kg bolus+infusion, fall 50–75/h, dextrose at 200–250), potassium (add once <5.2 + urine flows; total-body depletion), paediatric cerebral-oedema pitfall (slow osmotic shifts, bicarb only pH <6.9).
- 'c-imnci' — 6 layers: 5 general danger signs (any one = PINK), fast-breathing thresholds (<2 m ≥60 / 2–12 m ≥50 / 1–5 y ≥40, full-minute calm count), chest indrawing/grunting escalation, PINK pre-referral bundle (first-dose IM ampicillin + gentamicin, O₂, warmth, ORS sip, anti-hypoglycaemia → REFER), YELLOW facility care (oral amoxycillin 5 d), GREEN home care (no antibiotic; follow-up 2 d abx / 5 d fever / 30 d growth).
- 'c-thyroidstorm' — 6 layers: trigger → decompensation cascade (fever >40, AF, HF, gut chaos) → the 5-step block across 4 layers (β-blocker → PTU BEFORE iodine (TPO + 5′-deiodinase) → iodine 1 h later (Wolff-Chaikoff) → steroids + supportive) incl. Burch–Wartofsky ≥45.
- 'c-schizo-frs' — 6 layers: Schneiderian FRS quartet (thought alienation, 3rd-person/running-commentary voices, passivity, delusional perception — specific not sensitive), 6-month rule (≥1 mo active inside ≥6 mo + functional decline), positive vs negative layers, time-based differentials (brief <1 mo / schizophreniform 1–6 mo / delusional ≥1 mo / schizoaffective ≥2 wk psychosis-without-mood), treatment anchors (atypicals, clozapine ≥2 trials + agranulocytosis, EPS ladder).
- 'c-sepsis' — 6 layers: definitions (SOFA ≥2, septic shock MAP ≥65 + lactate >2, ~40% mortality), qSOFA vs obsolete SIRS, hour-1 lactate + cultures-before-antibiotics (≤45 min rule), broad-spectrum abx + 30 mL/kg crystalloid (hypotension/lactate ≥4), norepinephrine → MAP ≥65, source control 6–12 h + India anchors.
- Tints cycled from the existing family (#38bdf8, #34d399, #fbbf24, #fb7185, #a78bfa, #22d3ee, #f97316, #2dd4bf); 6 layers each with z 0..5; entries inserted after 'c-preec' before the closing brace; zero changes to existing entries or file formatting; no other file touched.

Task ID: 9
Agent: Z.ai Code (main)
Task: Investigate & fix "sign-in happens automatically" — make sign-in explicit, add real sign-out

Work Log:
- Investigated auth flow: `GET /api/profile` unconditionally returns the seeded demo account (single-account demo backend, no cookies/sessions); `page.tsx` hydration effect called it on every load and jumped straight into the app → landing + sign-in pages were skipped on every reload, and the app had NO sign-out anywhere. Sign-in was effectively dead code.
- Fix: introduced an explicit client-side session flag persisted in localStorage (`medos:session`):
  - `src/lib/store.ts`: added `SESSION_KEY`, `StoredSession`, `readStoredSession()`, `writeStoredSession(account)`, `clearStoredSession()`.
  - `src/app/page.tsx`: hydration now runs ONLY when a stored session exists — fresh visitors stay on the landing page; signed-in reloads resume the app (deep-link hash > last view > home).
  - `src/components/auth/signin-view.tsx`: writes the session on successful sign-in (both form and one-tap demo paths).
  - `src/components/profile/profile-view.tsx`: new "Account session" card (demo account badge + Active pill) with a Sign out button → clears session, clears store profile, routes to sign-in, toast confirmation. Last-view memory intentionally kept so the next sign-in resumes where the doctor left off.
  - Onboarding needs no change: it is only reachable after sign-in, so the session already exists.
- Verified with agent-browser E2E:
  1. Fresh visit + localStorage cleared → stays on landing page (no auto sign-in) ✅
  2. Sign in via "Use demo account" → enters app at #/home ✅
  3. Reload while signed in → resumes app directly (explicit session honored) ✅
  4. Profile → Account session → Sign out → sign-in screen, `medos:session` removed ✅
  5. Reload after sign-out → landing page again (no auto re-login) ✅
- `bun run lint`: 0 errors (only pre-existing seed.ts warning). dev.log clean during the whole flow.

Stage Summary:
- Sign-in is now an intentional act, not an automatic bypass. Session semantics: fresh browser → landing; explicit sign-in → app; reload → stays signed in; sign-out → returns to sign-in and stays out across reloads.
- Files touched: src/lib/store.ts, src/app/page.tsx, src/components/auth/signin-view.tsx, src/components/profile/profile-view.tsx.
- Note: the backend remains a single-account demo (GET /api/profile always returns the demo doctor). When real multi-user auth is added, replace the localStorage session flag with server sessions/cookies — the session gate in page.tsx is the single integration point.

- Discovered the 15-min webDevReview cron job from Task 17 (job #435034) did NOT survive the between-turn window (cron list returned total:0) — direct evidence that scheduled jobs do not survive sandbox restarts. Recreated it: fixed_rate 900s, job #435040, prompt now also instructs to run scripts/restore.sh if the dev server is down and to git-commit all work after each phase.
- Verified durability of everything else: working tree clean; latest code (incl. Task 17 nav changes, PARENT_OF map) present in git HEAD (auto-checkpoint commits); 264 files tracked; db/custom.db (672K, seeded demo data: profiles, attempts, sessions, revision items) is git-tracked → data survives resets; .gitignore only excludes build artifacts/logs (node_modules, .next, dev.log).
- Wrote scripts/restore.sh (idempotent, executable): bun install if node_modules missing → prisma generate → uses git-tracked db/custom.db as-is (only rebuilds from schema if the file is missing) → starts `bun run dev` in background if :3000 not answering → final HTTP health check with clear ✅/❌ output. Tested live: all steps green, server already up, 200 OK.
- Runtime state verified: next dev (v16.1.3) running on :3000, GET / → 200; no mini-services required by MEDULA (no websocket service in use).

Stage Summary:
- Recovery story is now: (1) code + DB live in git; (2) one command — `bash /home/z/my-project/scripts/restore.sh` — brings the runtime back; (3) the recreated 15-min webDevReview cron (#435040) both keeps improving the app and instructs future runs to re-check cron existence, git-commit work, and self-restore the dev server.
- Known limitation: platform-level scheduled jobs and running processes cannot be prevented from dying on a reset — but with git + restore.sh the sandbox can be rebuilt to identical working state in under a minute.

- Verification: fixed one self-inflicted syntax break (Part-2 lessons landed outside the lessons array; array closer removed, re-joined). `bunx tsc --noEmit | grep -E "packs/ai-medicine|curriculum/papers"` → EMPTY (registry.ts transient errors ignored per instructions). Dev server NOT run.

Task ID: 20
Agent: Z.ai Code (main)
Task: Recheck the sign-in page — "why automatically signed in?" Root-cause + fix: sign-in must be an explicit action, never silent.

Work Log:
- AUDIT: traced the full auth/session path. Backend = single-user demo (GET /api/profile always returns the seeded account); client-side session flag localStorage['medos:session'] written ONLY on explicit sign-in, cleared ONLY on Profile → Sign out. Root cause of the complaint was THREE compounding behaviors, all client-side:
  1. page.tsx boot effect: every reload with a stored session called api.getProfile() and dropped straight into the app — silent auto sign-in, zero interaction.
  2. Session never expired: the stored `at` timestamp was never checked — one demo login (even weeks ago, or from an old E2E run) stuck forever in that browser, so the sign-in page became unreachable without a manual sign-out.
  3. Landing "EXPLORE THE MEDICAL MAP" CTA: `if (readStoredSession()) setView('map')` silently bypassed sign-in entirely.
- FIX (Google account-picker pattern — explicit but one-tap):
  - store.ts: 30-day inactivity TTL in readStoredSession (expired/timestamp-less sessions are removed and treated as no session; writeStoredSession slides the window on each sign-in/resume).
  - signin-view.tsx: new "Welcome back, Doctor" resume mode. When a session exists the page shows: identity chip (Demo account · doctor@medula.in / Personal account), last-active relative time, optional "Picks up at {view}" chip, primary one-tap "Continue as Demo Doctor" (demo → POST /api/auth mode:demo; personal → GET /api/profile then routes to onboarding if never finished), and "Use a different account" (clears session → full form). Component now takes `initialHash` prop so Continue honors deep links (viewFromHash(initialHash) ?? readStoredView() ?? 'home').
  - page.tsx: boot effect no longer silently enters the app — with a session it routes to 'signin' (the resume gate). Deep-link hash captured via one-time useState (replaced useRef — lint react-hooks/refs forbids reading ref.current during render) and handed to SignInView. Removed now-unused api/viewFromHash/setProfile from Home.
  - landing-page.tsx: EXPLORE CTA always routes to 'signin' (the sign-in page itself decides resume-vs-form). No silent map entry.
- E2E (agent-browser, 11 scenarios, all PASS): fresh visitor → landing; EXPLORE CTA → form (was silent map entry); demo sign-in → dashboard; reload → resume gate (was auto sign-in); card shows identity + "Last active just now"; Continue → #/home; Learn + true reload → "Picks up at Learn" → Continue → #/learn; "Use a different account" → session null + form; no-session reload → landing; form sign-in works (resumes stored last view); Profile sign-out → session null + form; planted 31-day-old session → rejected + auto-removed → landing; mobile 390px resume card no overflow (scrollW=390); mobile Continue → app.
- Note: agent-browser hash-only `open` is same-document navigation (known gotcha) — used window.location.reload() to force true reloads in tests.
- Screenshots: agent-ctx/auth-resume-card.png, agent-ctx/auth-resume-desktop.png.

Stage Summary:
- Sign-in is now ALWAYS an explicit user action: no session = landing/full form; active session = one-tap "Welcome back" resume gate; 30-day TTL; deep links preserved through the gate; switch-account escape hatch. tsc src/ clean, lint 0 errors (2 pre-existing seed warnings), dev.log clean, no console errors. No backend changes; zero data touched.

Work Log:
- Inspection (spec mandate step 1): Question model (182 Qs; qtype sba 94/vignette 66/rapid 20/integrated 2; difficulty d1 42/d2 97/d3 39/d4 4; 161 concept-linked; tags EMPTY — no PYQ flags; 7 image-ish stems), QuestionAttempt (268 attempts/193 correct/69 error-typed; 9 error types + timeMs + confidence), KnowledgeState (57 states), ConfusionPair (17), engine.ts (scoreCandidate NEXT-BEST-ACTION weights already exist: recallRisk .30/weakness .28/exam .22/year .10/errors .10), /api/questions (static per-run selection: mix random|high-yield|weak + subject/system/topic/concept/pair filters), /api/attempts (transaction: attempt + KnowledgeState update → explanation/teaching/mastery), /api/attempts/error-type (ErrorPattern + confusion→revision automation), /api/mistakes, Question Lab UI (Practice/Mock/Mistakes tabs).
- Gap analysis vs PRODUCT 04: (1) no per-answer adaptive re-selection or difficulty staircase; (2) no "why this question" transparency; (3) no focus-lock after misses (random switching instead); (4) no PYQ-pattern tagging; (5) no image-based tagging; (6) no per-question timer in practice, no mark-for-review outside mocks; (7) no bookmark/report-question; (8) no option-level "why others are wrong" (explanation only) or AI per-question actions; (9) no session-level insight report (accuracy/speed/difficulty/strong-weak/recommended-next); (10) response time + confidence collected but never used for selection.
- DECISION: build as a SEPARATE first-class section `#/adaptive` ("Adaptive Engine", Target icon, Daily-study nav group) — existing Question Lab untouched. Deterministic measurable engine (lib/adaptive.ts), LLM only for on-demand AI actions (clearly labeled, never authoritative). No chain-of-thought exposure — engine emits only human "whyThis" notes.
- Shared contract written by main agent (no agent conflicts): types.ts += AdaptiveMode/ADAPTIVE_MODES/AdaptiveConfig/AdaptiveQuestion/AdaptiveSessionStart/AdaptiveAnswerFeedback/AdaptiveNextQuestion/AdaptiveTopicInsight/AdaptiveReport/AdaptiveHomePayload; store.ts += 'adaptive' View/APP_VIEWS/viewToLabel/adaptivePreset+setAdaptivePreset. tsc src/ clean.

Stage Summary:
- Contract frozen: POST /api/adaptive/session {config} → AdaptiveSessionStart; POST /api/adaptive/answer {sessionId,questionId,selected,timeMs,confidence,errorType?} → AdaptiveAnswerFeedback; POST /api/adaptive/next {sessionId} → AdaptiveNextQuestion (true AI-adaptive re-selection); POST /api/adaptive/complete {sessionId} → AdaptiveReport; GET /api/adaptive/home → AdaptiveHomePayload; POST /api/adaptive/ai {action: explain|simplify|similar|harder|easier|weakness} → structured (AI questions labeled non-authoritative); GET/POST/DELETE /api/adaptive/saved (bookmarks); POST /api/adaptive/report-question.
- Schema plan (additive, non-destructive): AdaptiveSession {mode,config,state Json,total,answered,correct,report?,completedAt?}, SavedQuestion (@@unique profile+question), QuestionReport {reason,detail}. Question.tags repurposed additively: curated subset gets 'pyq-pattern' / 'image-based' via a NEW update-only seeder (no fabricated exam/year claims — honest "PYQ-pattern" labeling).
- Engine plan: mode pools (weakness/pyq/rapid/clinical/image/exam/adaptive/custom) + weighted candidate scoring (weakness .30, recallRisk .25, errorHistory .20, exam .15, novelty .10) + difficulty staircase (2 right ↑ / 2 wrong ↓) + focus-lock (after a miss, next Q from same topic/adjacent edges) + whyThis from the winning weights.
- Next: Task 2-a (backend) + Task 2-b (frontend) in parallel with frozen contract.

KEY FIND: recordAttempt() (lib/attempt-record.ts) is the single shared attempt writer for ALL sources (practice, mock, PYQ, image, adaptive, tutor quizzes) → one capture hook covers everything.
- Schema (additive, db:push): MistakeRecord {profileId, questionId, conceptId?, wrongCount, firstWrongAt, lastWrongAt, lastSelected, lastErrorType?, status unresolved|revising|retested|resolved, retestCount, retestCorrect (consecutive 0..2), resolvedAt?, resolvedBy 'retest'|'manual', lastReviewedAt?} @@unique(profileId,questionId); relations added to Question + StudentProfile. 182 questions unchanged.
- Engine src/lib/mistake-intel.ts: (1) ensureMistakeRecords — groupBy sync of the attempt feed → backfills ALL historical wrongs into the bank (69 records materialized instantly on demo profile); (2) priorityFor — 0..100 from six measured factors each with a human note (frequency 25 · exam relevance 15 via platform pyq-pattern/difficulty/qtype tags · topic weakness 20 via KnowledgeState mastery · recency 10 · forgetting risk 20 via SRS estimatedRecall · careless risk 10 via fast-miss/overconfidence); (3) flags repeated/fast-miss/overconfident/today/forgotten/exam; (4) pattern detection — curated ConfusionPairs with both sides in the profile's errors ("You keep mixing up A and B" + mnemonic), repeated concepts (≥2 misses + mastery), subject×error-type PARADOX ("accuracy 73% in Micro but misreads leak — fix the habit, not the topic"), speed (median fast-miss), overconfidence, difficulty clustering; (5) review-mode predicates + sorts (today/repeated/impact/unresolved/forgotten/exam); (6) loadMistakeContext shared loader (records + question metas w/ loose topicId resolution + states + revision set + latest-wrong timing/confidence + subject stats + per-subject type breakdown).
- Capture hook: recordAttempt now upserts MistakeRecord on every WRONG answer (best-effort, never fails the attempt; correct answers leave records untouched — resolution is deliberate, never accidental). Verified live: wrong answer via classic /api/attempts → record created instantly w/ concept link.
- Routes /api/mistakes-intel/: home (genome: totals/doNext/patterns/counts/facets), list (mode + subject/type/difficulty filters), detail (full profile: options w/ isAnswer/isWrongPick + curated optionNotes, explanation, 6-factor breakdown, attempt history, matching ConfusionPair w/ points + mnemonic, drillAvailable; stamps lastReviewedAt), action (resolve/revising — revising also creates a REAL RevisionItem (dedup per concept, same rule as error-type automation) feeding the Revise engine/reopen), retest (same question fresh, attemptNo), retest/answer (graded through the SHARED recordAttempt — one knowledge engine; correct #1 → retested, consecutive #2 → resolved, wrong → unresolved + count bump via capture).

Task: Design + freeze the Smart Revision contract; prepare schema; orchestrate backend/frontend builds

Work Log:
- Inspection (spec mandate step 1): existing revision infra = /api/revision (due flashcards + due concepts + debt/counts), /api/revision/review (SM-2-lite srsUpdate on FlashcardReview), /api/revision/clear (clears RevisionItem + strengthens KnowledgeState + logs StudySession), revise-view.tsx (714 LOC flashcard flip deck + due-concept list). Signals available: KnowledgeState (score/stability/estRecall/lastReviewed/status ×60), QuestionAttempt (321, errorType/timeMs/confidence), MistakeRecord (70 open, with wrongCount/status lifecycle), RevisionItem (8 open, reason/priority/minutes), FlashcardReview (121 rows), ConfusionPair (17 curated), Question bank (182; tags pyq-pattern=30, image-based=13), ClinicalCase, Concept.summary/whyMatters/mnemonic/detail, Topic.importance + Subject.neetWeight, StudentProfile.examDate/examMode/dailyHours. GAPS: no unified measured daily queue, no per-block WHY, no revision modes, no session lifecycle, no exam-proximity shifting, no concept-level revision statuses.
- Contract FROZEN by main agent (no agent may edit these): types.ts += View 'revision' + RevisionMode/REVISION_MODES/RevisionBlockKind/REVISION_BLOCK_KIND_LABELS/RevisionWhy/RevisionBlock/RevisionExamClock/RevisionQueuePlan/Revision{Question,Concept,Flashcard,Pair,Case}Content/RevisionSessionContent/RevisionIntelligence/RevisionSmartHome/RevisionSessionStart/RevisionSessionResume/RevisionBlockResult/RevisionSessionSummary/RevisionAiResponse; store.ts += APP_VIEWS 'revision' + viewToLabel; api.ts += revisionHome/startRevisionSession/resumeRevisionSession/completeRevisionBlock/completeRevisionSession/revisionAi.
- Schema (additive, db:push OK, 182 questions unchanged): RevisionSession {profileId, mode, config Json, plan Json (block ids+reasons, NO answers — content re-hydrated on resume so answers never persist), total, done, minutes, status active|completed, createdAt, completedAt} + StudentProfile.revisionSessions. NOTE: SQLite rejects Json @default("{}") — fields are written on create (same pattern as AdaptiveSession).
- Engine design (src/lib/revision-engine.ts): loadRevisionContext(profile) ONE pass (states+concept/topic/subject, open RevisionItems, open MistakeRecords+question meta, due FlashcardReviews, attempts 30d per-concept accuracy + 'forgot' counts, confusion pairs, cases, exam clock) → buildPlan(ctx, mode, minutes, filters) PURE + deterministic. Candidate scoring per concept: 0.30*(1-recall) + 0.25*(1-mastery/100) + 0.15*examRelevance/5 + 0.15*mistakePressure + 0.10*topicImportance/5. Block kinds: concept (key facts from detail/summary/mnemonic), flashcards (batches of ≤10 due cards), mcq (3-5 weak-topic questions), pyq (PYQ-pattern), mistake (1 record, retested inline via /api/attempts → recordAttempt = ONE knowledge engine), compare (curated ConfusionPair with both sides in the student's wrong concepts), case (hand-off on weak system). Greedy fill by priority until minute budget. Exam proximity: examDate ≤60d or examMode → mistakes/pyq/weak boosted ×1.25, plan.exam.near true. Every block: reason sentence + why chips (recall est N%, missed N×, PYQ-pattern, mastery N%) — measured only, no chain-of-thought. Mode counts on home = blocks.length of a 20-min plan per mode (same builder, so counts are real queue sizes).
- Routes /api/revision/smart/: GET home (today plan default budget = clamp(dailyHours*60*0.5 → {10,15,20,30,45}), modes w/ measured counts, intelligence panel: overdue (recall<0.6) / forgotten (errorType 'forgot' ≥2 + low recall) / repeatedMistakes (wrongCount≥2) / strong / gaps (attempts>0 but never reviewed) / highRisk (weak × examWeight≥4), resume, recent, stats blocksToday+minutesThisWeek+lastRevisedAt, insufficientData) · POST session {mode,minutes?,subjects?,kinds?} → RevisionSessionStart {sessionId, plan, content} (content maps hydrated WITHOUT answer/explanation — grading always via /api/attempts) · GET session?id → resume (plan from stored snapshot + content re-hydrated) · POST block {sessionId, blockId, minutes?} → marks done; concept/compare blocks ALSO strengthen KnowledgeState (same blend formula as /api/revision/clear) + clear open RevisionItems for that concept + log StudySession(minutes,'revision'); flashcard/mcq/pyq/mistake blocks get their knowledge updates through the per-item endpoints · POST complete {sessionId} → summary (accuracy measured from QuestionAttempt rows in [createdAt, now] for the session's questionIds; next recommendation from remaining candidates) · POST ai {action: rapid-notes|recall|compare, conceptId?|pairId?} → z-ai SDK, GROUNDED in provided concept detail / ConfusionPair points (system prompt: use ONLY supplied context, refuse beyond scope, no citations/references, medical-content disclaimer), responses labelled aiGenerated + disclaimer. Existing /api/revision* routes and revise-view.tsx stay UNTOUCHED.
- Frontend design (src/components/revision/): smart-revision-view.tsx (home: hero "YOUR REVISION FOR TODAY" + headline chips + exam countdown, budget selector 10/15/20/30/45, queue list w/ kind icons + minutes + reason + why chips, "START REVISION" CTA, intelligence strip 6 statuses w/ expandable lists, 8 mode cards w/ measured counts, custom builder (subjects + kinds + minutes), resume banner, recent runs · run: block runner — concept block renders summary/whyMatters/keyFacts/detail sections + mnemonic + AI rapid-notes/recall panel + "MARK REVISED"; flashcards block reuses flip + AGAIN/HARD/GOOD/EASY grading via EXISTING /api/revision/review; mcq/pyq/mistake blocks render inline MCQ graded via EXISTING /api/attempts (AttemptResult feedback, error-type picker on miss) then next question; compare block two-column A/B + mnemonic; case block hand-off to Cases view · summary: blocks/minutes/accuracy/next recommendation) + revision-ai.tsx (disclaimered AI panel).
- Wiring plan: app-shell NAV + NAV_GROUPS (Daily study group, CalendarCheck icon), page.tsx render, MOBILE More sheet automatic.
- Next: Task 3-a (backend agent) + Task 3-b (frontend agent) in parallel against the frozen contract; main agent integrates, verifies E2E, writes final entry.

Stage Summary:
- Frozen contract + additive schema pushed (RevisionSession). Existing Revise section (flashcard SRS + debt list) preserved untouched — Smart Revision is a separate first-class section answering "what should I revise today and why", while Revise remains the card-deck surface. Flashcard grading + MCQ grading + mistake capture all reuse the EXISTING endpoints so every learning signal stays in one knowledge engine.

Old /api/graph?scope= route exists (legacy data feed for Doubt Search/map view) — left UNTOUCHED; Product 08 uses new /api/graph/* subpaths.
- Schema (additive, db:push non-destructive): ConceptEdge += source ("curated"|"ai-suggested"|"imported") + verified (default true) + @@index([type]); NEW GraphSynonym {id, term @unique, kind concept|topic|subject, refId, weight}; NEW GraphFeedback {profileId, fromId, toId, type, vote wrong|helpful|unsure, note, status open|reviewed|fixed}.

Result: 396 edges, 107 synonyms, idempotent (re-run = 0 created).

- Contract FROZEN in src/lib/types.ts: SimDifficulty + SIM_DIFFICULTY_META (beginner|mbbs|neetpg|advanced), SIM_SPECIALTIES, SimStageKind (patient|history|exam|investigations|differential|diagnosis|management|followup), SimInteractionKind (explore|key|choice|multi), SimOptionPublic/SimInteractionPublic/SimStagePublic/SimPatient/SimCaseSummary/SimCaseDetail, SimHome (stats/specialties/cases/weakAreas/repeatedErrors/recommended/recommendedDifficulty/recent/resume), SimFeedback (perOption verdicts correct|acceptable|wrong|harmful + missed essentials), SimActResponse, SimDebrief (timeline/learning/concepts/related graph groups/mistakeFed/handoffs), SimAiMessage/SimAiResponse.

- Prisma (additive, standalone engine model — no FK on profileId, db:push non-destructive): SimCase {id, title, specialty, system, difficulty(beginner|mbbs|neetpg|advanced), patient Json, brief Json = HIDDEN answer key (stages/interactions/verdicts/findings/keys/costs/learning/conceptIds/aiBrief), estimateMinutes, imageKey?, imageCaption, aiReady, source curated|imported-legacy}; SimCaseAttempt {profileId, caseId FK, status active|completed|abandoned, mode guided|ai, stageIndex, events Json (append-only {stageId, interactionId, chosen[], correct, score, ts, chat?}), score, diagnosisCorrect, reasoning, investigations, management, timeMs, startedAt, completedAt}. Brief NEVER leaves the server raw — routes strip verdicts/why/finding/key/cost.
- Content authored (prisma/seed-sim-cases-a.ts + -b.ts, ~2,600 LOC, ALL ORIGINAL): 14 curated cases covering ALL spec specialties: Medicine sim-gi-bleed (mbbs, endoscopy image, AI-ready), Surgery sim-appendicitis (beginner, AI-ready), Paediatrics sim-neojaundice (mbbs), ObGyn sim-pph (mbbs), Psychiatry sim-psychosis (mbbs), Dermatology sim-psoriasis (beginner), Ophthalmology sim-glaucoma (neetpg), ENT sim-epistaxis (beginner, AI-ready), Orthopaedics sim-compartment (neetpg), Radiology sim-freeair (neetpg, erect-CXR free air image), Pathology sim-leukaemia (advanced), Emergency Medicine sim-trauma (advanced, tension pneumothorax primary survey), Cardiology sim-chb (mbbs, CHB ECG image), Cardiology sim-pulmedema (neetpg, pulmonary oedema CXR image). Every case = 7-8 stages (Patient→History→Examination→Investigations→Differential→Diagnosis→Management→Follow-up), progressive reveal (history/exam/investigation EXPLORE multi-selects with per-item findings + key items + harmful traps), key-finding identification, result interpretation (3 with images + captions), differential shortlist (multi), diagnosis commit, management bundle (correct/acceptable/harmful with per-option whys), 2 follow-up questions; learning pearls + real concept links (verified against DB, all valid).
- Legacy import (prisma/seed-sim.ts): converter maps the 4 legacy cases' phases to stage kinds, keeps their question steps as choice interactions (teaching line = correct-option why), and appends authored differential/diagnosis/management stages (LEGACY_EXTRAS) so imported cases exercise the same reasoning loop. Graves/Nephrotic→mbbs, AMI/DKA→neetpg.
- Seeder run: 18 SimCase rows (16 specialties incl. legacy Endocrinology/Internal Medicine/Nephrology labels), 4 image cases, 3 AI-ready (aiBrief personas in briefs), concept links verified. package.json script db:seed-sim.

ENGINE RULES (binding for 9-a — measured, deterministic, no CoT):
- Interaction scoring: explore = 100*keysHit/keysTotal − 25*harmfulPicked (clamp 0..100; no keys → 100 unless harmful picked); key/choice = 100 correct / 60 acceptable / 0 wrong-or-harmful; multi = 100*correctPicked/correctTotal − 20*harmfulPicked (clamp).
- Stage weights: patient 0, history 15, exam 10, investigations 15, differential 15, diagnosis 25, management 15, followup 5 — total = weighted mean over PRESENT stages (0-100). reasoning = history+exam+differential+followup mean; investigations = investigations stage; management = management stage; diagnosisCorrect = the diagnosis-stage correct option chosen.
- Home numbers all MEASURED from SimCaseAttempt rows: completed/attempted, diagnosticAccuracy = %runs diagnosisCorrect, avgScore, minutesPractised = sum(timeMs)/60000, per-specialty accuracy, weakAreas = specialties/concepts <70% with ≥1 attempt, repeatedErrors = same interactionId missed (correct=false) in ≥2 runs, recommended next case (unattempted, specialty of lowest accuracy, difficulty = ladder step from recent accuracy ≥70%→up / <50%→down), resume = active attempt.
- Mistake Intelligence feed on complete: !diagnosisCorrect → ErrorPattern upsert (profileId, 'reasoning', primary conceptId) + RevisionItem (conceptId, reason 'Missed in case «title» — diagnosis was X', priority 3, minutes 10); correct diagnosis but reasoning <50 → ErrorPattern only; harmful management picks ≥2 → ErrorPattern 'confused'. StudySession(kind 'case', minutes = round(timeMs/60000), label case title) always logged. Max 1 RevisionItem per run.
- Debrief: timeline from events + brief feedback; learning; concepts with KnowledgeState mastery/status (primary first); related = knowledge-graph neighborsOf(primary) top 4 groups × 4 items (verified edges only — reuse the P08 engine); handoffs {conceptId, topicId (from concept), subjectCode}; mistakeFed honesty report.
- AI Case Mode (api/sim/ai): ZAI.create() pattern from api/graph/ai (JSON_RULE, loose-JSON parse + salvage, try/catch deterministic fallback from brief). System prompt = case aiBrief (persona/hidden/exam/investigations/style) + rules: stay in character as the patient, reveal ONLY facts asked for from the brief, never invent findings, never reveal the diagnosis even if asked, no medical advice, NO chain-of-thought (conclusions only), ≤80 words. Validates attempt (mode 'ai', active) + case aiReady; chat persisted as {type:'chat'} events in the attempt (cap 40 msgs, 600 chars each). DISCLAIMER: 'AI-generated educational scenario — practice tool, not real patient data or medical advice.' Badge: 'AI-GENERATED SCENARIO'. Commits (diagnosis/management) always graded by the DETERMINISTIC engine, never the LLM.
- Routes: GET /api/sim/home · GET /api/sim/cases/[id] (client-safe detail + resume) · POST /api/sim/cases/[id]/attempt {mode} · POST /api/sim/attempt/[id]/act {stageId, interactionId, chosen[]} · POST /api/sim/attempt/[id]/stage {stageIndex} · POST /api/sim/attempt/[id]/complete → SimDebrief · POST /api/sim/attempt/[id]/abandon · POST /api/sim/ai. All guards: 404 unknown, 409 double-complete, event-append idempotency (re-act same interaction = replace, not duplicate).
- Frontend targets (9-b): src/components/sim/ sim-view.tsx (home|player|debrief|ai machine) + sim-home.tsx (hero stats, specialty grid 12+ chips, difficulty filter+recommended chip, case cards with image/AI/best-score, weak areas, repeated errors, resume banner) + sim-player.tsx (stage rail, explore reveal cards w/ cost chips, key/choice/multi interactions, inline feedback after commit, image viewer w/ caption + ownership label, timer, quit-confirm w/ resume) + sim-debrief.tsx (scores ring, decision timeline w/ verdicts, learning, concept chips → openHub/openConcept, 'Practice MCQs' via setAdaptivePreset autoStart, 'Add to revision' note, repeat) + sim-ai.tsx ('You are the doctor — I am the patient' chat + AI-GENERATED badge + structured commit panel reusing diagnosis/management interactions). app-shell: relabel 'cases' → 'Case Simulator' (Stethoscope stays, group 'Clinical & deep dives'). page.tsx render + simFocus consumption.

Stage Summary:
- PRODUCT 09 data layer + content library COMPLETE: 18 cases (14 curated incl. all 12 spec specialties + 4 preserved legacy), 4 image-based (platform-owned images), 3 AI-ready, difficulty ladder beginner→advanced, hidden answer keys server-side only, additive non-destructive schema. Contract frozen for 9-a/9-b; DoD path: enter case with limited info → think/investigate/diagnose/decide → clear educational feedback → related concepts → MCQs → revision.

- Prisma (additive, standalone — profileId plain string, db:push non-destructive): LabImage {id,title,diagnosis,modality,system,subjectCode,conceptIds,src,provenance,sourceNote,difficulty 1-3,examRelevance 1-5,isNormal,compareGroup,brief Json HIDDEN,aiReady}; LabAttempt {profileId,imageId FK,mode,status,stepIndex,answers Json append-only,correct,score,findingsHit/Total,timeMs}. db:push OK.

ENGINE RULES (binding for 10-a — measured, deterministic, no CoT):
- Grade: identify/diagnose = 100 correct / 60 acceptable / 0 wrong; interpret locate = pin hit if hypot(((dx/100)*aspect)²+(dy/100)²) ≤ r/100 (aspect = natural w/h from client, clamped 0.2..5), locate score = 100*hits/locatable, labels = 100*presentPicked/presentTotal − 20*absentPicked clamp 0-100, interpret step = mean(locate,labels); quiz per-q 100/60/0 → mean; rapid per item correct → clamp(100 − 4*floor(timeMs/1000), 40, 100) else 0 (server clamps reported timeMs 300..30000), session = mean over items.
- Home all MEASURED from LabAttempt: accuracy = graded completed correct %, interpretationCoverage = pins hit %, avgTimeMs, rapidBest = max rapid score, weakModalities <70% ≥1 graded, missedPatterns = same finding label missed ≥2 runs, recommended = unattempted image in weakest modality → any unattempted → lowest last score; rapidPoolSize = graded images.
- Mistake feed on complete: wrong graded identify/diagnose/rapid → ErrorPattern('visual', primary concept) + RevisionItem (priority 3, minutes 10, reason 'Missed image "<title>" — answer was <diagnosis>'); interpret coverage <50 → pattern only; StudySession kind 'lab' minutes max(1, round(timeMs/60000)) ALWAYS; max 1 RevisionItem per run.
- Debrief: steps timeline (verdicts+whys+pins revealed), teaching, concepts w/ KnowledgeState mastery, related via loadGraphContext+neighborsOf (P08), mistakeFed honest, handoffs {conceptId, topicId, subjectCode}, nextImages = same modality pool (6).
- Routes: GET /api/lab/home · GET /api/lab/images/[id] (+resume) · POST /api/lab/images/[id]/attempt {mode} (auto-abandons stale active for same image) · POST /api/lab/attempt/[id]/act {stepId, chosen?, pin?, aspect?, timeMs?} (validates ids, clamps, idempotent replace, 400 on re-act after done) · POST /api/lab/attempt/[id]/stage {stepIndex} · POST /api/lab/attempt/[id]/complete (409 double) · POST /api/lab/attempt/[id]/abandon (409 if completed) · POST /api/lab/rapid {modality?} (10 items least-attempted, attempt.imageId = first) · POST /api/lab/ai {imageId, question} — grounded ONLY in brief (findings labels/descriptions/whys, diagnosis, teaching, sourceNote) + similar images' briefs for compare; uncertainty framing mandatory; never definitive diagnosis language; no chain-of-thought; ≤130 words; loose-JSON {reply} salvage; deterministic fallback from guided steps; badge 'AI-GENERATED · EDUCATIONAL'.
- Brief stripping: identify/diagnose/quiz answers, verdicts, whys, regions NEVER leave the server in detail payloads — only {id,label} projections + guided steps (teaching content) + counts. Pins graded server-side only.
- Content mapping (frozen): 16 images → seed-lab-a.ts (7 owned-clinical: boot-heart→c-tof, free-air→c-ulcer, left-collapse→c-cxr, pulm-edema→c-heartfail, CHB→c-arrhythmia, gastric-ulcer→c-ulcer+c-hpylori, graves-scan→c-graves; 9 diagram: stemi→c-ami+c-ecg, af→c-arrhythmia, hyperk→c-hyperk, cxr-normal→c-cxr isNormal, ptx→c-cxr, miliary→c-tb, mca→c2-medicine-stroke, sdh→c-trauma-primary+c2-medicine-stroke, ms→no concept) · 15 images → seed-lab-b.ts (sickle→c2-pathology-anemias, aml→c-leukemia+c2-pathology-leukaemias, psoriasis→c-psoriasis+c2-derm-psoriasis, zoster→none, fundus-htn→c-htn, fundus-dr→c-diabretino+c-dm, cataract→c2-opht-cataract, gram-staph/ecoli→c2-microbiology-gram-stain, colles→c-colles+c2-orth-colles, achalasia→none, gallstone→c-cholecystitis, granuloma→c-tb+c-inflamm, cirrhosis→c2-pathology-cirrhosis, coronary→c-coronary). compareGroups: cxr-batwing {normal,pulm-edema}, ct-head {mca,sdh,ms}, smear {sickle,aml}, derm {psoriasis,zoster}, fundus {htn,dr}, gram {staph,ecoli}.
- Frontend targets (10-b): src/components/lab/ lab-view.tsx (home|study|player|debrief machine + labFocus) · lab-home.tsx (loop headline «See → Identify → Interpret → Reason → Learn → Practice», stats, rapid-fire banner w/ scope, filters subject/system/modality/difficulty/exam-relevance, image cards w/ provenance chips, weak modalities, missed patterns, recommended, recent, resume) · lab-viewer.tsx (zoom/pan/pinch/double-tap wheel viewer, pin layer %, reveal-annotations toggle w/ numbered markers, compare mode 2-up) · lab-study.tsx (viewer + guided sequential reveal + 6 mode launchers + AI panel + similar) · lab-player.tsx (identify/interpret[locate→label]/diagnose/quiz/rapid runners w/ inline feedback + timer + quit-confirm) · lab-debrief.tsx (ring, timeline, teaching, concepts→openHub, related, mistakeFed, nextImages→openLab, Practice MCQs→setAdaptivePreset+setView('adaptive')).

Stage Summary:
- PRODUCT 10 data layer + verified asset library COMPLETE: 31 platform-owned teaching images across ALL 12 spec modalities with exact-region server-side pin grading, provenance honesty (3 tiers), additive non-destructive schema. Contract frozen for 10-content-a/b + 10-a/10-b. Image QA lesson recorded: AI photorealistic anatomy FAILED QA — deterministic SVG diagrams are the platform's accuracy backbone.

- Personalization uses measured platform data only and names it naturally in speech («You struggled with autonomic pharmacology recently…» style, fed by weak areas/revision due/accuracy). Medical accuracy: platform-content grounding, mandatory uncertainty phrasing, educational-only safety block; the tutor never presents itself as a replacement for a qualified professional.
- Progress feeds: StudySession (Performance Analytics), ErrorPattern + RevisionItem (Mistake Intelligence / Smart Revision), all surfaced back through voice home suggestions and the standard debrief hand-offs. Chain-of-thought never leaves the server (thinking disabled, voiceeval/[SESSION_END] stripped server-side).
- DoD met: put the phone down → Listen (greeting speaks) → Answer (speak or type) → Discuss → Get corrected → Revise → Improve, with every signal flowing back into the platform's existing intelligence.

- libraryHome: stats (platform 168 / external 37 / saved / verifiedExternal 35 measured); kinds histogram over the FULL combined catalog; top-12 subjects by combined count; sources grouped by sourceSlug w/ SOURCE_REGISTRY names + verified = every-entry-urlVerified (cdc honestly false); featured = 6 deterministic first-found ids (guideline ext:who-guidelines, course ext:openstax-ap, images ext:radiopaedia, reference ext:statpearls, pyq ext:nbems-neetpg, + highest-yield topic platform question-set platform:topic-mcqs:t-phys-cardcycle); recentTopics = LearnProgress(kind topic, updatedAt desc, 3) → registry topics, fallback = most-attempted Question topics in 7d via attempt→question→concept→topic join (measured); forYou ONLY on real signal (≥3 weak KnowledgeStates <60 OR ≥1 due RevisionItem OR ≥1 recent topic) — deterministic personalisation scoring (weak topic +4 / due topic +2 / recent +1.5 / weak subject +2 / neetpg +0.5), reasonTags 'Covers your weak concept X' / 'Strengthens X' / 'Matches your recent topic X', max 2 per kind, 5 picks, honest reason line from measured basis; no signal → forYou null, hasSignal false.
- topicFeed / savedList / toggleSaved / reportResource / libraryAi: feed = topic-locked externals ranked before subject-level + all measured platform entries; savedList resolves every id (ext catalog / platform re-synthesis), drops unresolvable, savedAt = createdAt ISO; toggleSaved hardened beyond the letter of the spec — platform ids must RESOLVE against live counts (found via E2E: 'platform:bogus:x' was being saved; now 400 'Unknown resourceId'), ext ids must be in CATALOG_IDS; report validates reason ∈ {incorrect,broken,outdated,copyright,other}, resourceId prefix ext:/platform:, details clamped 500; libraryAi (backend-only ZAI, temperature 0.3, JSON_RULE + loose-JSON salvage pattern from api/sim/ai): recommend feeds top-12 candidates scored by the SAME personalisation scoring + metadata-only JSON (id/kind/title/subjects/difficulty/access/counts — externals also license+verified), validates picks ∈ candidate set (invented ids dropped → deterministic top-3 fallback), key-points/compare get ONLY our own description+metadata with explicit never-invent rules and ≤80 words, deterministic metadata fallbacks on SDK failure; badge 'AI-ASSISTED SUMMARY' + mandated copyright disclaimer verbatim.
- Routes (all force-dynamic, NextResponse.json, try/catch → 500 {error}, whitelisted params via readJson/asTrimmed/asInt): GET home · GET resources · GET resources/[id] (404) · GET+POST saved (400 invalid id) · POST report (400 invalid reason/id) · GET for-topic/[topicId] (404) · POST ai (400 unknown mode / unresolvable resourceIds). No writes to src/components/** or page.tsx.
